			BAC 2020 mate: tabel formule derivate si integrale		</a>
</h2>
<div class="postmetadata">
<span class="text-by">De</span> <span class="author vcard"><a class="url fn n" href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/author/profesorjitaruionel/" rel="author" title="Vezi toate posturile scrise de Profesor Jitaru Ionel">Profesor Jitaru Ionel</a></span> <span class="text-on">pe</span> <a href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/10/23/bac-2020-mate-tabel-formule-derivate-si-integrale/" rel="bookmark" title="17:52"><time class="entry-date" datetime="2019-10-23T17:52:36+02:00">23/10/2019</time></a> <span class="sep">•</span> <span class="commentcount">( <a href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/10/23/bac-2020-mate-tabel-formule-derivate-si-integrale/#respond">Scrie un comentariu</a> )</span>

<section class="entry">

<p>Exemple, formule și exerciții rezolvate cu DERIVATE (matematică, liceu, recapitulare BAC) Aveți în acest articol definiția derivatei într-un punct (formula cu limita)– exercițiu întâlnit des la BAC la III.1(a) , ecuația tangentei într-un punct de abscisă […]</p>

<a href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/19/exercitii-rezolvate-cu-multimi-clasa-a-6-a-test-de-evaluare/" rel="bookmark">
			Exercitii rezolvate cu MULTIMI (clasa a 6-a) +TESTE de evaluare		</a>
</h2>
<div class="postmetadata">
<span class="text-by">De</span> <span class="author vcard"><a class="url fn n" href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/author/profesorjitaruionel/" rel="author" title="Vezi toate posturile scrise de Profesor Jitaru Ionel">Profesor Jitaru Ionel</a></span> <span class="text-on">pe</span> <a href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/19/exercitii-rezolvate-cu-multimi-clasa-a-6-a-test-de-evaluare/" rel="bookmark" title="06:46"><time class="entry-date" datetime="2019-09-19T06:46:43+02:00">19/09/2019</time></a> <span class="sep">•</span> <span class="commentcount">( <a href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/19/exercitii-rezolvate-cu-multimi-clasa-a-6-a-test-de-evaluare/#respond">Scrie un comentariu</a> )</span>

<section class="entry">
<a class="thumbnail" href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/19/exercitii-rezolvate-cu-multimi-clasa-a-6-a-test-de-evaluare/">
<img alt="" class="attachment-broadsheet-archive size-broadsheet-archive wp-post-image" data-attachment-id="29066" data-comments-opened="1" data-image-description="" data-image-meta='{"aperture":"0","credit":"","camera":"","caption":"","created_timestamp":"0","copyright":"","focal_length":"0","iso":"0","shutter_speed":"0","title":"","orientation":"0"}' data-image-title="multimi" data-large-file="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?fit=630%2C501&amp;ssl=1" data-medium-file="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?fit=300%2C239&amp;ssl=1" data-orig-file="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?fit=630%2C501&amp;ssl=1" data-orig-size="630,501" data-permalink="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/19/exercitii-rezolvate-cu-multimi-clasa-a-6-a-test-de-evaluare/multimi-3/" height="80" sizes="(max-width: 100px) 100vw, 100px" src="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?resize=100%2C80&amp;ssl=1" srcset="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?w=630&amp;ssl=1 630w, https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?resize=300%2C239&amp;ssl=1 300w, https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/multimi-1.jpg?resize=100%2C80&amp;ssl=1 100w" width="100"/> </a>
<p>CLASA a 6-a (matematică, algebră, gimnaziu) -EXERCIȚII REZOLVATE CU MULȚIMI +TESTE: Recapitulare și 2 TESTE din CAPITOLUL MULȚIMI: descriere şi notaţii, elementele unei mulţimi, relaţia dintre element şi multime, relaţia dintre două mulţimi […]</p>

			Lectii cu exercitii rezolvate matematica LICEU (+pregatire/ recapitulare BAC)		</a>

<section class="entry">
<a class="thumbnail" href="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/13/siruri-marginite-siruri-monotone-teorie-si-exercitii-rezolvate-matematica-liceu/">
<img alt="" class="attachment-broadsheet-archive size-broadsheet-archive wp-post-image" data-attachment-id="28974" data-comments-opened="1" data-image-description="" data-image-meta='{"aperture":"0","credit":"","camera":"","caption":"","created_timestamp":"0","copyright":"","focal_length":"0","iso":"0","shutter_speed":"0","title":"","orientation":"0"}' data-image-title="Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2" data-large-file="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?fit=791%2C1024&amp;ssl=1" data-medium-file="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?fit=232%2C300&amp;ssl=1" data-orig-file="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?fit=1700%2C2200&amp;ssl=1" data-orig-size="1700,2200" data-permalink="https://profesorjitaruionel.com/2019/09/13/siruri-marginite-siruri-monotone-teorie-si-exercitii-rezolvate-matematica-liceu/analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2/" height="80" sizes="(max-width: 100px) 100vw, 100px" src="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?resize=100%2C80&amp;ssl=1" srcset="https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?resize=100%2C80&amp;ssl=1 100w, https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?zoom=2&amp;resize=100%2C80&amp;ssl=1 200w, https://i2.wp.com/profesorjitaruionel.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/Analiza-matematica-fisa-de-recapitulare-siruri-2.jpg?zoom=3&amp;resize=100%2C80&amp;ssl=1 300w" width="100"/> </a>
<p>ȘIRURI MĂRGINITE DEF 1: Se spune că șirul (xn) este mărginit superior (MAJORAT) dacă există b∈R astfel încât xn≤b, ∀n. DEF 2: Se spune că șirul (xn) este mărginit inferior (MINORAT) dacă există a∈R […]</p>

<p>ADUNAREA NUMERELOR NATURALE -Exemple de calcul rapid: 1). Observă exemplele de calcul rapid: 25+9=25+10-1=35-1=34 68+11=68+10+1=78+1=79 137+99=137+100-1=237-1=236 642+102=642+100+2=742+2=744 5798+998=5798+1000-2=6798-2=6796 2). CALCULEAZĂ ASEMĂNĂTOR: 38+9=… 117+99=… 4387+999=… 77+8=… 265+98=… 7735+998=… 69+11=… 256+101=… 3195+1001=… 87+12=… 546+102=… […]</p>

				jQuery( document ).ready( function( $ ) {
					var orderByDefault = 'relevance',
						orderDefault   = 'DESC',
						widgetId       = decodeURIComponent( 'jetpack-search-filters-5' ),
						searchQuery    = decodeURIComponent( '' ),
						isSearch       = 0;

					var container = $( '#' + widgetId + '-wrapper' ),
						form = container.find('.jetpack-search-form form'),
						orderBy = form.find( 'input[name=orderby]'),
						order = form.find( 'input[name=order]'),
						searchInput = form.find( 'input[name="s"]' );

					orderBy.val( orderByDefault );
					order.val( orderDefault );

					// Some themes don't set the search query, which results in the query being lost
					// when doing a sort selection. So, if the query isn't set, let's set it now. This approach
					// is chosen over running a regex over HTML for every search query performed.
					if ( isSearch && ! searchInput.val() ) {
						searchInput.val( searchQuery );
					}

					searchInput.addClass( 'show-placeholder' );

					container.find( '.jetpack-search-sort' ).change( function( event ) {
						var values  = event.target.value.split( '|' );
						orderBy.val( values[0] );
						order.val( values[1] );

						form.submit();
					});
				} );
